## 10.1 Files you can safely ignore at first

| Path | Size (wc -l) | Why you can skip it |
|------|-------------:|---------------------|
| `src/pub/*` | 5 files, 627 lines | Generated protobuf/gRPC stubs (`*_pb2.py`, `*_pb2_grpc.py`) from `src/protos/*.proto`. Read the `.proto` files instead; regenerate, do not edit. |
| `src/**/tests/*` | 86 `.py` files, 18,131 lines | Unit tests. Useful later as executable examples, but several are stale per the surveys (chat and prompt_builder slices). |
| `src/components/prompt_builder/constants.py` | 1247 | Mostly prompt text (seed templates, interface guidance). Only `:1-191` holds code-relevant limits and the medium appendix. |
| `src/components/skin_analysis/fonts/Roboto-Regular.ttf`, `models/face_detection_yunet_2023mar.onnx` | binary (515 KB, 233 KB) | Font for annotated images and the OpenCV YuNet face model. Loaded by `image_annotation_renderer.py` and `face_detector.py`; never read by hand. |
| `src/components/skin_analysis/scripts/*` | 2 files, 123 lines | Developer scripts (`check_scans.py`, `simulate.py`); not imported by the service. |
| `scripts/synth_greeting.py` | 104 | One-off greeting synthesis helper; needs the voice provider registry to understand. |
| `src/components/voice/worker_local.py` | 84 | Local-development variant of `worker.py`; not used by Docker or Kubernetes. |
| `alembic/versions/*` | 34 files, 3,006 lines | Schema history. Read a migration only when you change a table; `src/database/constants.py` and the model files give the current shape. |
| `src/components/rbac/*` | 4 files, 104 lines | Not imported by any non-test module (grep verified); dead permission helpers. |
| `src/core/voice_container.py` | 42 | Only imported by one test (`src/components/prompt_builder/tests/test_builder_stream5.py:593`); the real container is `src/core/container.py`. |
| `src/components/db_constants/*` | 17 files, 1,276 lines | Constants seeding that depends on the missing `digital_assets` submodule; read only after the submodule is checked out. |

# 11. Known gaps, risks and contradictions found during mapping

This section lists what the twelve survey readers and the orchestrator found wrong, dead, duplicated or inconsistent while mapping the code. It is deduplicated and consolidated from 366 survey risk items and 122 survey contradiction items into the 120 most important findings. Each bullet names the problem, says why it matters, and cites the file and line. Secret VALUES are never written here; secrets are named by their environment variable name only. Line numbers were spot-checked against the checkout on 2026-10-10 (40 citations re-read with `sed -n`; two were corrected and are noted inline). Items whose lines were not re-read are marked "(not re-verified)".

## 11.1 Secrets and security

- **Hardcoded non-empty secret defaults in source** — `src/core/environment.py` ships real-looking default values (redacted here) for CDR_WEBHOOK_RELAY_SECRET (`src/core/environment.py:37-40`), MAKUNAI_STREAM_SECRET (`:121-123`), GROQ_API_KEY (`:138-140`) and GNANI_API_KEY (`:146-149`); comments at `:33-36` call them temporary. None of these four keys exist in the dev Kubernetes Secret, so the hardcoded values are what actually runs in dev. `docs/architecture/05_CODING_STANDARDS.md:143` forbids exactly this. Placeholder passwords also ship as non-empty defaults: MONGO_PASSWORD (`src/core/environment.py:67`), POSTGRES_PASSWORD (`:74`), CACHE_PASSWORD (`:83`), WHATSAPP_API_TOKEN, WHATSAPP_VERIFY_TOKEN and WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET (`:125-127`) default to non-empty strings; OPENAI_API_KEY (`:100`) and GEMINI_API_KEY (`:143`) default to the empty string (re-verified; the survey's claim of non-empty defaults was wrong); `src/config/settings.py:9` defines `oauth_token_secret` with a non-empty default.
- **Plaintext Kubernetes Secret committed to git** — `oc-config/dev/makun-ai-env-secret.yaml` (name `makun-ai-env-secret`, namespace `maglo-integration`, 56 keys at `:7-63`) holds database passwords, LiveKit, OpenAI, Gemini, Deepgram, Cartesia and Sarvam keys and the credential master key in `stringData`, and ArgoCD auto-syncs it (`oc-config/argocd/apps/makun-ai-dev.yaml:19-22`).
- **TLS certificate verification disabled for Redis** — `ENV.CACHE_SSL_OPTIONS` returns `{"ssl_cert_reqs": ssl.CERT_NONE}` for `rediss://` (`src/core/environment.py:355-359`), and the resulting warning is silenced globally at import (`src/makunai_celery/celery_utils.py:7-11`).
- **WebSocket runs outside every HTTP middleware** — `WS /ai/v1/voice/stream/{agent_id}` (`src/components/voice/controllers.py:403`) is never seen by AuthMiddleware because `BaseHTTPMiddleware` passes non-"http" scopes straight through (verified, `starlette/middleware/base.py:98-101`). The only check is the HMAC ticket in `verify_stream_token` (`src/components/voice/platform/stream_token.py:45-66`); the ticket travels as `?token=` in the query string (`controllers.py:425`), `partner_id` is a caller-asserted query parameter (`:424`), and the ticket is neither partner-scoped nor single-use although `docs/architecture/07_PSTN_SIP_RELAY.md:28` calls it single-use.
- **Cross-tenant read on GET agent** — `GET /ai/v1/agents/{agent_id}` accepts an optional `partner_id` query parameter that replaces the authenticated partner (`src/components/agents/controllers.py:317-324`).
- **Agent secrets and session tokens** — `GET /ai/v1/agents/{id}/secret` returns the decrypted secret (`src/components/agents/services.py:800-805`) although the POST description says plaintext is returned only once (`controllers.py:470,570`, not re-verified); `POST /ai/v1/agents/session` stores `makun_ai:session_token:{token}` in Redis (`services.py:834-835`) but nothing reads it, because AuthMiddleware only understands API-KEY, X-Agent-Secret and Bearer (`src/middlewares/authentication.py:57-82`).
- **Provider and OAuth secrets stored in plaintext** — partner LLM keys live unencrypted in `makun_ai_partner_configs.meta_config` (`src/components/partner_config/models.py:66-70`) and are mirrored into Redis (`src/components/partner_config/services.py:271`), with no TTL on the Postgres-fallback path (`src/components/llm/factory.py:195-199`); OAuth client secrets sit in `integration_templates.client_config` JSONB (`src/components/integrations/models.py:70`) and `GET /ai/v1/integrations/templates/{id}` returns them to any authenticated partner user (`src/components/integrations/controllers/templates.py:150-158`).
- **Missing authorization scoping** — `POST /ai/v1/integrations/templates` and `POST /ai/v1/integrations/mcp/discover` modify provider-wide templates with no admin check (`src/components/integrations/controllers/templates.py:166-210,213-259`); any partner can set `is_global=True` on a tool, making it visible to every partner (`src/components/tools/schemas.py:44`; `src/components/tools/repositories.py:84`); RAG progress/cancel/pause are keyed only by `task_id` (`src/components/rag/controllers.py:182-207`); `DELETE /agents/{agent_id}/routes/{route_id}` ignores the URL agent (`src/components/agents/repositories.py:774-779`), as do `PATCH /{integration_id}/config` and `set_credential_mode` (`src/components/integrations/services/agent_integrations.py:459-466,508-511`); constants endpoints have no partner scoping (`src/components/db_constants/controllers.py:129-130,190-192`).
- **LLM-controlled input reaches outbound HTTP calls unescaped** — `HttpGenericAdapter` reads token, API key, username and password from the model-supplied args before the stored credentials (`src/components/integrations/adapters/http_generic.py:242,257,274-275`); `request_body_template` is rendered with `SandboxedEnvironment(autoescape=False)` then `json.loads`-ed (`:22,313-322`); `tool.api_endpoint.format(**parsed_arguments)` interpolates into the URL without encoding (`src/components/tools/executor.py:1080`); the "prompt-injection sanitizer" strips five substrings such as `###` (`src/components/tools/constants.py:181-188`). The SSRF guard is applied only on MCP discovery: `url_safety` is applied in `McpDcrService` only; template and integration `base_url` values used by `HttpGenericAdapter` and `McpAdapter` are never validated, and the guard does no DNS resolution (`src/components/integrations/core/url_safety.py:1-8`; `adapters/http_generic.py:69-70`; `adapters/mcp_generic.py:45-51`).
- **OAuth callback posts to a wildcard origin** — the unauthenticated callback page (excluded at `src/middlewares/authentication.py:41`) calls `window.opener.postMessage(..., '*')` (`src/components/integrations/controllers/oauth_callbacks.py:150-156`).
- **Secrets and PII logged at INFO** — `RequestResponseLoggingMiddleware` logs all request headers (Authorization, API-KEY, X-Agent-Secret) and full bodies (`src/middlewares/logging_request_and_response.py:62-71,113-121`); `AuthServiceClient` logs the raw bearer token (`src/grpc_client/client_services/auth_service_client.py:22-26`); `_handle_api_key` logs the key on failure (`src/middlewares/authentication.py:161-163`); the Celery broker URL containing CACHE_PASSWORD is logged on every app creation (`src/makunai_celery/celery_utils.py:27-29`); webhook handlers log full raw bodies (`src/components/campaign/controllers/webhooks.py:204-208`); tool executor debug logs print partially masked secrets (`src/components/tools/executor.py:1143-1174,1233-1248`).
- **Webhooks use shared secrets, not signatures** — `babbler-reply` compares `X-Webhook-Secret` to BABBLER_WEBHOOK_SECRET (`src/components/campaign/controllers/webhooks.py:48`); `tata-dialer-cdr` compares to CDR_WEBHOOK_RELAY_SECRET (`:193-200`); the legacy `holler-cdr` route (`:146`) is still live. The partner holler API key is cached in Redis in plaintext for 15 minutes (`src/components/campaign/services/dispatch.py:925-927`).
- **Fail-open limits and weak upload validation** — the agent-secret rate limiter returns "allowed" on any Redis error and is keyed only by agent_id (`src/components/agents/secret_rate_limit.py:26-31`); `TRUST_PROXY_HEADERS` is hardcoded `True` (`src/components/chat/constants.py:45`), so `X-Forwarded-For` can evade the per-IP iframe budget unless the edge proxy overwrites it. Upload validation is weak: text MIME types skip magic-byte sniffing, unknown signatures are treated as compatible, and the whole file is read into memory before any size check (`src/components/chat/media/helpers.py:101,176-177,213-216`; `src/components/chat/media/services.py:134`).

## 11.2 Configuration and environment

- **Unused `curses` import breaks Windows** — `src/core/environment.py:1` is `from curses.ascii import US`; `US` is never used, and on Windows the import raises `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named '_curses'`, so every module that imports `ENV` (the API, alembic, all workers) fails to import. Reproduced in the scratch venv; the Linux Docker image is unaffected.
- **Three environment-name conventions** — `ENV.ENVIRONMENT` reads env var `ENV` (`src/core/environment.py:54`) and is compared to `'PROD'` (`src/config/__init__.py:15`) and to `'local'` (`src/main.py:48`); `AppEnvironment` reads a different env var `Env` with values int/local/testing/staging/production (`src/core/app_env.py:24`); `src/grpc_client/constants.py:1-7` defines prod/staging/dev/local/test/int (unused); email constants accept PROD/PRODUCTION (`src/components/email_service/constants.py:23`); `src/middlewares/exception.py:16-25` keys on DEV/PROD; `ROOM_NAME_PREFIX` derives from env var `ENV` (`src/components/voice/constants/session.py:10-13`) and `_is_pstn_call` tests `startswith('call-')`, which also matches the `call-int`/`call-local` prefixes (`src/components/voice/worker.py:697`). Nothing in `.env.example` or the dev Secret sets `Env`. The root log level is read from an env var literally named `PROD`: `src/config/log.py:3` does `os.environ.get('PROD','INFO')`; `LOGGING_CONFIG` is only applied by `BaseLogger._load_dict_conf`, whose call is commented out (`src/loggers/base.py:35,79`); `ENV.LOGGING_CONFIG_PATH` (`src/core/environment.py:78`) is never read.
- **`validate_env_vars` quirks** — it runs at import only when env var `ENV` is set (`src/core/environment.py:414-415`) and then raises for ANY empty attribute not in `allowed_empty` (`:370-411`), including LIVEKIT_API_KEY, DEEPGRAM_API_KEY, OPENAI_API_KEY, GEMINI_API_KEY, DO_*, MAILMG_* and TTS_VOICE_ID (default `None`, `:104`); SARVAM_API_KEY's placeholder default satisfies the check (`:322`).
- **ENV surface drift: stale `.env.example` and dead attributes** — `.env.example` lists GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID/SECRET, GOOGLE_REDIRECT_URI, CALENDAR_ENCRYPTION_KEY, CALENDAR_STATE_SECRET and DASHBOARD_BASE_URL, which no code reads (`.env.example:16-21,29-34`), but omits LIVEKIT_*, OPENAI_API_KEY, DEEPGRAM/CARTESIA/GEMINI keys, TTS_VOICE_ID, MAILMG_* and every HOT_TIER_*/COMPRESSION_*/MAX_SESSION_* knob; its MONGO_URI uses port 27017 while ENV defaults to 27016 (`src/core/environment.py:57`). Conversely, DO_ACCESS_KEY_ID, DO_ENDPOINT_URL, DO_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY, DO_SPACE_NAME, STORAGE_SERVICE_PROVIDER (possibly used by the missing submodule), FRONTEND_INTEGRATION_URL, GRPC_CA, GRPC_CA_CERT_PATH, GRPC_CERT_PATH, GRPC_KEY_PATH, LLM_CONNECT_TIMEOUT, LLM_POOL_TIMEOUT, LLM_WRITE_TIMEOUT and HOT_TIER_TTL_SECONDS are defined but never read (`src/core/environment.py:43-55,60-62,125-127,177,179-184,362-364`, not re-verified); `ENV.IDEMPOTENCY_TTL_SECONDS` is read at `src/components/chat/services.py:580` but never defined, so the TTL is always 300. In the ops repo, DB_CONNECTION_URL, GOOGLE_CALENDAR_* and VAULT_ENCRYPTION_KEY exist in the dev Secret but nothing in `src/` reads them (not re-verified).
- **`LocalSettings` is required everywhere** — `src/config/settings.py:47` instantiates `LocalSettings()` at import, so the five required fields at `:15-19` (MONGO_DB/USER/PASSWORD/HOST/PORT) must be set even by processes that never touch Mongo; `ProdSettings` (`:43-49`) lacks every field `LocalSettings` has. `load_dotenv` is called twice with different discovery strategies (`src/core/environment.py:7`; `src/config/__init__.py:10-13`).
- **UTF-8 byte-order marks** — `src/components/common/pagination.py` and `src/components/sessions/controllers.py` start with bytes EF BB BF (verified with `od`).
- **Config read outside ENV** — OPENAI_API_KEY is read via `os.environ` in the voice worker (`src/components/voice/worker.py:330` vs `:139,443`), the skin engines (`src/components/skin_analysis/engines/factory.py:71,80`) and the OpenAI provider (`src/components/llm/providers/openai_llm.py:58`); ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is not in `ENV` at all and `os.environ[...]` raises KeyError if missing (`src/components/llm/providers/anthropic_llm.py:64`); CREDENTIAL_MASTER_KEY_V{n} and CREDENTIAL_MASTER_KEY_CURRENT_VERSION come from `os.environ`, the version at import time (`src/components/integrations/core/static_methods.py:29-30`; `src/components/integrations/constants.py:110-112`); GREETING_CACHE_DIR, GREETING_TTS_FAST_PROVIDERS and GREETING_TTS_NATIVE are read in `src/components/voice/session/ws_relay_session.py:737,1015,1237,1266`; OAUTH_AUTH_URL_{PROVIDER} is an undocumented override (`src/components/integrations/services/credentials.py:207`). None of these are seen by `validate_env_vars`.
- **Corrupted `.gitignore` entry** — line 176 is `rag_dump.dump` encoded as UTF-16 (null bytes between characters, verified with `od`); the correct entry repeats at `.gitignore:182`.
- **Redis and Mongo URL helpers ignore their inputs** — `RedisCache.init_redis_pool(host, port, password)` discards all three and rebuilds the URL from `ENV.CACHE_*` (`src/core/redis.py:19-27`), returns a client rather than a pool and closes with the deprecated `.close()` (`:30`); `src/makunai_celery/celery_config.py:11` computes an unused MONGO_URI while `ENV.MONGO_URI` (`src/core/environment.py:57`) is defined differently.

## 11.3 Build, CI and deployment

- **CI never runs tests and the workflows disagree on the branch** — `.github/workflows/build.yaml:49-63` has the pytest/coverage steps commented out; the workflow only does `poetry install`. `build.yaml:5` triggers on `master` and `feature-dev-2026`; `deployment.yaml:6` triggers on `feature--dev--2026` (double hyphens). Both use deprecated `actions/checkout@v2` and `setup-python@v2`, and a PAT is injected globally via `git config url.insteadOf` (`deployment.yaml:15,22,28`, not re-verified).
- **k8s manifest mismatches with oc-config** — `k8s/campaign-dispatch-worker.yaml:35` uses image `makunai-service:latest` while CI pushes `<registry>/makun-ai:integrationlatest` (`.github/workflows/deployment.yaml:49`); `:49` and `:107` reference Secret `makunai-service-env`, but the only Secret in the ops repo is `makun-ai-env-secret` (`oc-config/dev/makun-ai-env-secret.yaml:4`). The KEDA scaler needs an env var nobody defines: both ScaledObjects use `addressFromEnv: REDIS_ADDRESS` (`k8s/campaign-dispatch-worker.yaml:70,128`); REDIS_ADDRESS exists neither in `ENV` nor in the Secret.
- **No Deployment for the API, general worker or beat** — the ArgoCD Application syncs only `oc-config/dev/`, which contains just the Secret (`oc-config/argocd/apps/makun-ai-dev.yaml:13-18`); `k8s/` in the main repo is referenced by nothing.
- **Dockerfile oddities** — `poetry install --without local` excludes an empty group (the "local only" packages are main dependencies, `pyproject.toml:54-61`); `EXPOSE 7000 7001 50051 9090` but nothing binds 7001 or 50051 (`Dockerfile:6`); `COPY . ./` ships tests, docs and alembic (`Dockerfile:21`) (not re-verified).
- **docker-compose oddities** — obsolete `version: '3'` (`docker-compose.yml:1`); `makunai-service` publishes 50050, which nothing listens on (`:10`); the livekit worker block is duplicated as a commented copy (`:170-189`); requires a pre-created external network `makunai_console` (`:196-200`); the general `celery-worker` has no `-Q`, so it also consumes the two campaign queues the dedicated workers are meant to isolate (`:28` vs `:50,:68`; verified, `celery/app/amqp.py:168-216`).
- **Grace period does not cover the hard time limit** — `terminationGracePeriodSeconds=60` is justified by `soft_time_limit=25`, but `dispatch_campaign_tick` has `time_limit=40` and retries re-run the full tick (`k8s/campaign-dispatch-worker.yaml:28-32`; `src/components/campaign/tasks.py:315`) (not re-verified).
- **Python version and dependency drift** — Dockerfile pins `python:3.11-slim-bookworm`, `pyproject.toml:8` allows `<3.15`, but the code imports stdlib `audioop` (removed in 3.13) in `src/components/voice/session/lifecycle.py:4`, `ws_relay_session.py:76`, `adapters/tts/greeting_pool.py:4` and `scripts/synth_greeting.py:21`, and no `audioop-lts` is locked. `pyproject.toml` also pins `anthropic ^0.21.3` (very old) next to `openai ^2`, keeps the archived `aioredis ^2.0.1` alongside `redis 5.3.1`, leaves `livekit-plugins-turn-detector = "*"` unpinned and lists `grpcio-tools` (a build tool) as a runtime dependency (`:18,21,24-25,44,47`, not re-verified).
- **Empty README and stale doc index** — `README.md` at the repo root is empty; `docs/architecture/README.md:9-15` indexes only documents 01-04 (05 and 07 are not listed and there is no 06); the oc-config README is a template placeholder.

## 11.4 Database and migrations

- **Missing submodule breaks alembic and the app** — `src/components/digital_assets` is declared in `.gitmodules` but empty; `alembic/env.py:17` imports `src.components.digital_assets.models`, and `src/core/container.py:89,95` plus 14 other files under `src/` import from it (16 importing files in total, grep verified), so neither migrations nor the app import from this checkout (verified).
- **Two `declarative_base()` objects** — `src/core/db.py:17` (used by `src/database/base.py:5` and `alembic/env.py:66`) and `src/core/base.py:3` (no importers). A third base-like class, `ConstantsBase`, redefines id/created_by/updated_by/description columns that `MetaData` already provides (`src/components/db_constants/models.py:15-22` vs `src/database/base.py:11-16`).
- **Duplicate and conflicting table-name constants** — "Campaign module tables (Dev 2)" at `src/database/constants.py:37-44` (`CAMPAIGN_TABLE='makun_ai_campaigns'`, `LEAD_LIST_TABLE`, ...) versus the "Dev 1" names actually used (`CAMPAIGN_TABLE_NAME='makun_ai_voice_campaigns'`, `CAMPAIGN_LEAD_LIST_TABLE_NAME`, ...) at `:45-59`; the Dev 2 block is unused.
- **Broken downgrades and migration churn** — `op.drop_constraint(None, ...)` for foreign keys created without a name (`alembic/versions/daefe2473245_v6.py:55,62`; `cc12e9a1a891_v13.py:60,67`); SQLAlchemy raises when the constraint name is `None` (verified, `sqlalchemy/sql/compiler.py:7074-7085`). Migration churn: v24 adds `recipients.system_error_count`, v25 drops it (`e1a2b3c4d5f6_v24.py:172-181`; `f6a7b8c9d0e1_v25.py:63`); v29 creates `ix_campaign_recipients_dialing_partial` inside `try/except Exception: pass` and v30 drops it again (`b7c8d9e0f1a2_v29.py:36-45`; `0d05e3f46a90_v30.py:50`); v22 silently removes column comments v20 added (`27a90e705533_v22.py:334-349`); v26 is an empty no-op (`0c6937ff15f1_v26.py`) (not re-verified).
- **Tables without the `makun_ai_` prefix** — `agent_invocation_log` and `agent_invocation_findings` (`alembic/versions/d94bf1acfc63_v17.py:24,68`) are the only unprefixed tables; `AgentInvocationLog` stores `parent_agent_id`/`sub_agent_id` as `String(36)` while callers pass ints, and `AgentInvocationFindings` ends up with a composite primary key (`src/components/orchestration/models.py:21,27-28,46-50`).
- **Mixed time units** — `AgentRoute` soft delete writes `deleted_at = int(time.time())` (seconds) while `MetaData.created_at/updated_at` are epoch milliseconds (`src/components/agents/repositories.py:772,786`; `src/database/base.py:17-21`).
- **Three Postgres engines and three Mongo clients in one API process** — module-level `postgres_session_manager` (`src/core/db.py:76-79`), the Container `db` Singleton (`src/core/container.py:17-24`) and `sql_session_factory` in `src/main.py:37-53`; `mongo_database_manager` at import (`src/core/db_mongo.py:31`), `DocDatabaseSessionManager` in the Container (`container.py:901`) and a third `MongoDatabaseManager()` as `main.py:37` `session_factory`, which no executed code uses. `?ssl=require` is silently overridden: `src/main.py:46-50` appends `?ssl=require` outside local, but `DatabaseSessionManager` passes `connect_args={'ssl': None}` (`src/core/db.py:25`), and `connect_args` wins (verified, `sqlalchemy/engine/create.py:633-634`).
- **Mongo indexes never created** — `MongoHistoryManager.ensure_indexes` and `ChatTranscriptRepository.ensure_chat_transcript_indexes` have no callers (`src/components/history_manager/manager.py:209`; `src/components/chat/repositories.py:293`; startup at `src/main.py:58` does not call them).
- **Constants loader bugs** — `update_constant` tests the class-level column attribute, which is always truthy, so `created_by` is never recorded (`src/components/db_constants/repositories.py:170-174`); `dynamic_variable.py` runs a SQL select through the Mongo `session_factory` imported from `src.main` (`src/components/db_constants/loader/dynamic_variable.py:8,26-36`); `DbConstants` reads its dict once at startup, so `PUT /constants` changes are invisible until restart (`src/components/db_constants/initializer.py:170-181`).
- **`interface_overrides` has no column** — `TemplateCreateRequest`/`TemplateUpdateRequest` accept and validate `interface_overrides` and `interface_override_mode`, but `AgentPromptTemplate` has no such columns and no migration adds them (`src/components/prompt_builder/schemas.py:113-116,146-148`; `models.py:40-123`; `repositories.py:355-380,396-406`); the prompt-template seed also inserts on every replica start without conflict handling (`template_initializer.py:188-206`).

## 11.5 Dead, duplicated or confusing code

- **Container duplicates and a disabled dependency check** — `lead_list_repository` and `voice_dnc_repository` (and their factory functions) are defined twice; `lead_list_service` captured the first object and `campaign_service` the second, so two Singleton instances of each repository exist (`src/core/container.py:486-489,689-692,498-501,661-664,1176-1178,1289-1291`) (not re-verified). `Container.check_dependencies` is overridden to `pass` (`src/core/container.py:1373-1374`), while `src/main.py:25` still calls it as if it validated something.
- **Dead modules** — `VoiceContainer` (`src/core/voice_container.py:5-42`) is never used by the worker (`src/components/voice/worker.py:487` builds plain `Container()`); `src/components/rbac/` and `src/components/common/user_hierarchy.py` have zero importers; `src/exceptions/validation.py:6` imports a module that does not exist (`src.components.common.response`) and is never imported, so no custom `RequestValidationError` handler is registered; `src/components/llm/provider.py:7` imports names that do not exist in `constants.py` and has no importers; `src/components/voice/worker_local.py` has no references.
- **Duplicated constant block** — `TOOL_ALLOWLIST_CONFIG_KEY`, `TOOL_NAME_ALIAS_MAP` and `OUT_OF_SCOPE_TOOL_MESSAGE` are defined twice with the same comment header (`src/components/agents/constants.py:120-143` and `:187-210`); `DEFAULT_MAX_TOKENS = 2048` at `src/components/llm/constants.py:4` is overridden by an import of the agents constant (=1024) at `:7`.
- **Two parallel LLM exception hierarchies** — `src/components/llm/errors.py` (`LLMException`, `MissingPartnerApiKeyError`) and `src/components/llm/exceptions.py:77` (`LLMBaseError`, `MissingPartnerApiKeyError`); `src/components/chat/services.py:75,734` and `src/components/voice/session/lifecycle.py:114` catch the `errors.py` classes while providers raise the `exceptions.py` ones, so those handlers never match.
- **Logger name collision and reversed level map** — `BaseLogger._type()` returns `cls.__class__.__name__`, which is `'type'` for every subclass, so all loggers share one stdlib logger and each `get_logger()` adds another handler pair, duplicating log lines (`src/loggers/base.py:27-44,73-75,85-93`; verified by snippet); `LogLevel.get_log_level` maps 4 to CRITICAL and 5 to ERROR, the reverse of its own enum (`src/loggers/__init__.py:9-30`).
- **Dead disabled two-stage dispatch** — `USE_TWO_STAGE_DISPATCH = False` (`src/components/campaign/constants.py:200`) makes `dispatch_placement_batch` (`src/components/campaign/tasks.py:408-457`), `reserve_one`/`schedule_batch`/`execute_placement_batch` (`services/dispatch.py:416-696`) and `CAMPAIGN_PLACEMENT_QUEUE` (`constants.py:196`) dead; the task has no route (`src/makunai_celery/celery_config.py:24-27`).
- **Shadow, canary and A/B paths left in production code** — `'TEMP A/B TEST (2026-10-01)'` under `ENV.INT_RECOVERY_FEATURES_ENABLED` (`src/components/voice/session/event_router.py:330-335,556-567`); `ENV.RUNTIME_PORT_CANARY_PERCENTAGE` randomly routes between `self.runtime.on` and `self.session.on` (`:357`) and the canary runtime duplicates the legacy builder (`src/components/voice/pipeline/builder.py:96-125` vs `:155-196`); `LLMCoreStream` and `LLMTurnCore` are near-duplicate turn implementations gated by `ENV.LLM_TURN_CORE_SHADOW_ENABLED` (`src/components/voice/adapters/llm_adapter.py:383-965` vs `adapters/llm_core_bridge.py:48-621`, gate at `llm_adapter.py:321`); the new `TranscriptEngine` runs as a shadow and its `TurnAction` verdict is discarded (`src/components/voice/session/handler.py:989-990` vs `src/components/voice/transcript/engine.py:118-229`).
- **Duplicate names and copy-pasted helpers** — three classes named `TextNormalizer` (`src/components/voice/transcript/normalizer.py:4` static; `transcript/core/normalizer.py:38` instance; `src/components/common/helpers.py:4`); two named `TranscriptCandidate` (`transcript/guard.py:11-19` vs `transcript/core/candidate.py:13-70`); two named `PartnerConfig` (pydantic `src/components/llm/schemas.py:73` vs the SQLAlchemy model imported by `src/components/llm/factory.py:23`); campaign str-Enums in `constants.py` duplicated as plain classes in `src/components/campaign/enums.py:21-63`; sessions `SessionStatus`/`EndReason` are plain classes, not Enums (`src/components/sessions/constants.py:4-20`). `_clean_messages` is copy-pasted three times: OpenAI, Groq and Gemini providers each carry it and mutate caller dicts in place (`src/components/llm/providers/openai_llm.py:233-289`; `groq_llm.py:261-314`; `gemini_llm.py:268-321`).
- **Reach-through and inverted dependencies** — `dispatch_coordinator` reads `_DispatchService__redis` via `getattr` (`src/components/campaign/tasks.py:239,296`); the voice session layer reaches into `engine._strategy/_normalizer/_buffer/_guard` (`src/components/voice/session/handler.py:469-477,848,1011,1075`), `session._activity`, `_tts`, `_stt` (`lifecycle.py:531-533,810,959`) and `core._context_orchestrator._session_memory` (`llm_adapter.py:845-895`); `KnowledgeBaseRetriever` uses `VectorRepository._session_factory` (`src/components/rag/retrieval/knowledge_base_retriever.py:129,182`). Package dependencies are also inverted and circular: `src/components/voice/ports/__init__.py:1-8` and `ports/runtime.py:5` import from `adapters/`; `tools` and `integrations` import each other in both directions (`src/components/tools/clients/mcp_client.py:8-20`; `src/components/integrations/adapters/mcp_generic.py:12`); `src/components/db_constants/loader/dynamic_variable.py:8` imports `src.main`.
- **Leftover debugging artefacts** — stream-of-consciousness comments (`'Wait, if we want to clear it...'` at `src/components/integrations/repositories/partner_integrations.py:338-341`; `'Wait, the original logic: ...'` plus an empty `pass` branch at `src/components/voice/transcript/core/capture_fields/phone.py:86-90`); `print()` in the voice commit gate (`src/components/voice/transcript/guards/profile_guard.py:125-150`), startup (`src/main.py:58-87`) and the constants initializer (`src/components/db_constants/initializer.py:58,109-128`); dev scripts inside the package that call the paid image API or read private attributes (`src/components/skin_analysis/scripts/simulate.py:24-29,47`; `src/components/skin_analysis/scripts/check_scans.py:9-12`).
- **Misleading annotations and unused imports** — `get_partner_id: int = get_partner_id` (a function) at `src/components/agents/controllers.py:65-66` and in every other controller class; `CurrentUserMap.TEAM_LEAD_ROLE_HIERARCHY_LEVEL: int` has no value (`src/components/common/constants.py:16`); `src/components/voice/services.py:112,518` annotates an `AgentConfig` as `dict`; unused imports and undefined annotation names appear in most packages (e.g. `src/components/agents/services.py:3,8-10,15-17`; `src/components/chat/controllers.py:3,5,14,21,27-29,34,149`; `src/components/rag/tasks.py:2,7,16,217,273`; `src/components/voice/pipeline/builder.py:19-23,26`). Undefined names in local annotations do not raise because function-local annotations are not evaluated (verified by snippet).
- **Stale tests** — `src/components/chat/tests/test_rate_limits.py:282,284` references `ChatService._cached_event_from_dict`, which moved; most `src/components/prompt_builder/tests/*` import names that no longer exist (`test_builder_stream5.py:26-38`, `test_renderer.py:17-20`, `test_services.py:36-40`); the transcript slice has zero tests; only one unit test touches Celery (`src/components/campaign/tests/test_fallback_tasks.py`).

## 11.6 Chat/LLM/context specifics

- **Anthropic provider incompatible with the locked SDK** — `AnthropicProvider` dispatches on event class names `RawContentBlockDeltaEvent` etc. (`src/components/llm/providers/anthropic_llm.py:160-180`), but anthropic 0.21.3 only defines `ContentBlockDeltaEvent`/`ContentBlockStartEvent`/`MessageDeltaEvent`, and `messages.stream()` has no `tools` kwarg (verified, venv `anthropic/resources/messages.py:1500-1529`); finish reasons `end_turn`/`tool_use` fail the `Literal` validation of `EndOfTurnData.finish_reason` (`src/components/llm/schemas.py:229-234`; raised at `core.py:509`); history is passed in OpenAI shape with role `tool` (`anthropic_llm.py:141-146`).
- **LLM flags never forwarded** — every `EndOfTurnEvent` omits `had_tools` (`src/components/llm/core.py:438,450,462,474,509-513`) although voice adapters rely on it to bypass caches (`src/components/voice/adapters/llm_adapter.py:512`; `llm_core_bridge.py:144`); `LLMCore` calls `provider.stream()` without `voice_mode` (`core.py:549-555`), so the `VOICE_MAX_TOKENS=120` cap is dead on the main path; `LLMRequest.orchestration_enabled/interface/medium/prompt_source` are set by `ChatService` but never read by core or engine (`src/components/llm/schemas.py:158-185`).
- **Context-overflow guard uses the wrong lookup** — `_guard_context_overflow` uses `MODEL_CTX_LIMITS.get(model, 128_000)` instead of `get_model_ctx_limit()`, so the per-prefix limits are never applied (`src/components/llm/core.py:1298`; `constants.py:170-197`); `ContextOverflowError` is raised before the `try` (`core.py:323` vs `:368`) so it is never converted to fallback text and closes the session as `error`.
- **Turn-loop exits, delegation checks and a fake RAG tool call** — several `run_turn` exits end without `EndOfTurnEvent` (turn budget `src/components/llm/engine.py:177-181`, tool-call limit `:239-240`, APPROVAL `:519-527`, transfer with no target `:567,578`, HANDOFF `:549-559`, not re-verified); `DelegationMode` is compared with the string `'SUB_AGENT'`, which is not a member (`engine.py:505-510`; `src/components/tools/constants.py:34-39`). `_sync_rag_context_to_db` writes a synthetic `knowledgebase_search` tool_call plus a `tool_execution_logs` row on every RAG turn, which `_sanitize_history` later strips (`src/components/llm/engine.py:989-1058`; `core.py:1034-1057`).
- **Latent `TypeError`s in the context layer** — `_fetch_rag` returns a bare `[]` on exception (`src/components/context/context_orchestrator.py:292`) while `:111` unpacks `(rag_docs, rag_citations)`; `RedisHotTier.append(session_id, message)` (`src/components/context/hot_tier.py:50`) is called with three positional args by `LLMCore` (`src/components/llm/core.py:343-345,502-504`), harmless today only because the container never passes `hot_tier` to `LLMCore`.
- **Compression can never trigger with defaults** — transcripts are trimmed to `MAX_SESSION_MESSAGES=50` (`src/components/chat/repositories.py:190`) while `compress_session` requires 60 uncompressed turns (`src/core/environment.py:181-182`; `src/components/context/tasks.py:40-41`); trimming also shifts the array positions compression uses as `original_index` (`src/components/context/repository.py:101,143-149`); when enabled, a `compress_session` message is enqueued on every append (`src/components/history_manager/manager.py:149-169`).
- **Hot tier ignores the medium policy and `sliding_window`** — when warm, up to `HOT_TIER_MAX_TURNS=50` raw turns are returned instead of 5 (TEXT) / 8 (VOICE); `ContextRequest.sliding_window` is never read (`src/components/context/context_orchestrator.py:185-200`; `hot_tier.py:21,38`; `schemas.py:45`); the agent-level `sliding_window` is accepted by `AgentCreate` but never stored or carried into `AgentConfig`, so 15 always applies (`src/components/agents/repositories.py:205-223`; `src/components/agents/services.py:1066-1090`).
- **Stream errors hidden behind HTTP 200** — rate-limit and lock errors raised inside the SSE generator become an `error` event on a 200 stream and the controller's `except` blocks are unreachable (`src/components/chat/services.py:605-620,729-758`; `src/components/chat/controllers.py:157,171-195`); `cached_events` collected before a mid-stream error are persisted in `finally` and replayed on retry without a `done` event (`services.py:575-584,706-717`).
- **Session lock and consent resume** — the lock is keyed by `session_id` only with a fixed 120 s TTL (`src/components/chat/constants.py:37-38,48-49`; `services.py:364-384`); consent resume uses the transcript's first-insert `agent_id` and rebuilds the request without interface/medium/context_data (`src/components/chat/controllers.py:215-223`; `src/components/chat/repositories.py:192-197`).
- **Attachment TTL contradicts its own comment** — `media/constants.py:62` says "No TTL is set" but `_write_metadata` sets 7 days (`src/components/chat/media/services.py:41,466-470`); after expiry history degrades to a text placeholder (`content_blocks.py:216-226`).
- **Session memory and fire-and-forget tasks** — `MemoryDetail` has no `confidence` field, so eviction sorts on a missing key (`src/components/context/session_memory.py:14-18,111`); importing `session_memory` imports the Celery app and instantiates a `Container()` (`:8,147`; `src/makunai_celery/celery_app.py:32`); `asyncio.create_task` results are dropped without a reference in `context_orchestrator.py:209-212`, `src/components/history_manager/manager.py:141-144,199-202`, `src/components/llm/engine.py:912-1058`, `src/components/prompt_builder/services.py:238-240` and `src/components/voice/session/lifecycle.py:192-227`.
- **Inconsistent prompt builders and unevicted provider caches** — `build_llm_request` applies context injection, voice instructions and interface context; `build_llm_request_with_rag_context` and `_with_inject` skip all of them (`src/components/agents/services.py:326-372` vs `434-457,489-522`). Provider caches are keyed by raw API key and never evicted: `_provider_cache` and `_shared_clients` in `src/components/llm/factory.py:121-150`, `openai_llm.py:50`, `groq_llm.py:69`, `gemini_llm.py:68`; the factory falls back to `Container().partner_config_repository()`, instantiating a new container (`factory.py:181-185`).

## 11.7 Tools/integrations specifics

- **`ToolAlreadyExistsError` constructed with the wrong arity** — `__init__(tool_name, agent_id)` (`src/components/tools/errors.py:32`) but called with `tool_name` only (`src/components/tools/repositories.py:257,320`), so a duplicate name yields a `TypeError` and HTTP 500 instead of 400.
- **Accepted but unimplemented modes** — INTERNAL tools with delegation mode TRANSFER force `handler_name='TRANSFER'`, but `_handle_orchestration_tool` implements only `delegate_to_agent`; `COMPLETE_TASK` and `remember_detail` are likewise unhandled (`src/components/tools/executor.py:1294-1296,1309-1321,1413,1506-1511`; `src/components/orchestration/constants.py:41-48`); the OAUTH2 credential type is accepted on create but `ToolAuthResolver` returns no auth for it (`src/components/tools/auth_resolver.py:251-252`; `src/components/tools/credentials/constants.py:25`).
- **Persistence quirks** — integration tool rows are shared across agents: the upsert key is partner + tool_name + target_service with no agent_id, so the last sync wins for `delegation_mode` (`src/components/integrations/repositories/partner_integrations.py:429-436,475-487`). `ToolExecutionLog.session_id` is NOT NULL but callers may pass `None`; the `IntegrityError` is only logged (`src/components/tools/models.py:217-222`; `src/components/tools/executor.py:586-604`); the executor builds a brand-new `Container()` to resolve `integration_service` when not injected (`executor.py:221-229`).
- **Inconsistent defaults** — MCP discovery defaults every non-readOnly tool to APPROVAL, HTTP templates to SILENT (`src/components/integrations/services/agent_integrations.py:979-983` vs `:1863-1865`); `HttpGenericAdapter` defaults to BEARER when no auth config exists (`adapters/http_generic.py:222-232`); tool creation sets `is_active = is_published` and a PUT with `is_published=True` silently activates (`src/components/tools/repositories.py:277-278,327-328`).
- **Guesses, hardcoded redirects and dead code** — `CREDENTIAL_SWEEP_REFRESH_MARGIN_MS` is "assumed until console team confirms" (`src/components/integrations/constants.py:177-183`) and the sweep never runs anyway (see 11.9); the localhost OAuth redirect is hardcoded three times (`constants.py:14`; `src/core/environment.py:63-66`; `src/components/integrations/mcp/dcr_service.py:281-285`). Dead OAuth and WhatsApp code: `OAuth2Client.discover/register_client/exchange_token` (`src/components/integrations/core/oauth2_client.py:28-88`), `BaseIntegrationAdapter._get/_post/_patch/_delete` (`adapters/base.py:60-143`) and `assert_integration_not_already_enabled` (`repositories/partner_integrations.py:126-149`) have no callers; `src/components/integrations/controllers/templates.py:1-10,43-44` imports `hashlib`, `hmac`, `ChatMessageRequest`, `ChatService` and defines `_WA_ATTACHMENT_TYPES`/`_WA_SILENT_TYPES` for a `/whatsapp/webhook` route that does not exist (grep verified).
- **gRPC channel per call and an awaited sync function** — `RPCServiceFactory.get_service` creates a new channel and client on every call and never closes it (`src/grpc_client/rpc_service_factory.py:16-21`); `ErrorHandlingMiddleware` awaits this sync function, raising `TypeError` that is swallowed, so error e-mails never carry user details (`src/middlewares/exception.py:113-116`).
- **AuthMiddleware cache quirks** — `request.state.hierarchy` is set only on Redis cache miss; the `api_key:{key}` cache is read but never written, so API-KEY auth always hits gRPC; the "Inactive API Key" branch is unreachable (`src/middlewares/authentication.py:108-131,150-172`; `src/grpc_client/client_services/api_key_service_client.py:26-27`).
- **`/health` requires authentication** — `GET /health` (`src/components/health/controller.py:125`) is not in `excluded_paths` (`src/middlewares/authentication.py:34-49`), so unauthenticated probes get 401; the check connects to Mongo without credentials and `check_celery` is a copy of `check_redis` (`health/controller.py:31-50,73-92`).
- **Error e-mails only from two hardcoded public URLs** — `ErrorHandlingMiddleware` mails only when `str(request.url)` starts with one of two base URLs, to hardcoded personal addresses, using sync `requests.post` inside the event loop (`src/middlewares/exception.py:16-27,60-88`; `src/middlewares/constants.py:1-8`; `src/components/email_service/services.py:168`) (not re-verified).

## 11.8 Voice specifics

- **`NameError` in the OpenAI realtime adapter** — `src/components/voice/adapters/realtime/openai_realtime.py:50` uses `ENV`, which is not imported in `:1-16` (verified: the only `ENV` hit in the file is line 50).
- **API misuse caught against the venv** — `agent_speech_started`/`agent_speech_stopped` are subscribed at `src/components/voice/session/event_router.py:358-362,372-373` but do not exist in livekit-agents 1.8.2 (verified, venv `livekit/agents/voice/events.py:291-308`). `mint_ws_ticket` is not `@inject`-ed, so its `logger` dependency stays a `Provide` marker, which returns itself when called (`src/components/voice/controllers.py:372-378,397`; verified, venv `dependency_injector/wiring.py:1061-1062`).
- **LiveKit configuration built but unused** — `AgentSession` receives `turn_handling` endpointing keys it ignores (`src/components/voice/pipeline/builder.py:494-502`; `adapters/livekit/livekit_runtime.py:51`; verified, venv `livekit/agents/voice/turn.py:260-290`); the `MultilingualModel` turn detector is constructed (a HuggingFace download) but never passed to `AgentSession` (`builder.py:173-193`; `livekit_runtime.py:54-74`).
- **DTLN noise suppression wired nowhere** — referenced in profiles, worker preload and builder logs, but the only call site is commented out (`src/components/voice/session/lifecycle.py:278-286`); `handler.py:60-62` docstring and `docs/architecture/02_COMPONENT_DEEP_DIVE.md:383,421` say it is active.
- **Legacy Mongo fallback references missing attributes** — `self._container` and `self._logger` do not exist on `LLMCoreStream` (`src/components/voice/adapters/llm_adapter.py:747,755`); `has_immutable_chat_context = 'gemini' and '3.1' in model` contradicts the plugin's declared capability (`src/components/voice/session/agent.py:270-287,604-619` vs `adapters/realtime/gemini_realtime.py:44-47`, not re-verified).
- **Explicit `end_call()` skips finalization** — sets `_finalize_started=True` after `end_session`, so campaign transcript export and history drain never run (`src/components/voice/session/handler.py:329-337` vs `lifecycle.py:1490-1498`); `VoiceService.end_session` only finalizes the `CallSession`, deletes the room and marks the chat session idle (`src/components/voice/services.py:291-385`).
- **Relay calls have no `CallSession` or billing row** — `call_record_id=0` left as a to-do comment in source (`src/components/voice/session/ws_relay_session.py:473`); deferred-feature list at `:54-70`; the cascaded path lacks greeting cache, pacer and `TranscriptEngine` (`:1480-1521`).
- **Three greeting cache key schemes and module-level state** — sha256 Redis key in `lifecycle.py:1118-1131` (must match `services.py:484-502`), sha1 file digest in `ws_relay_session.py:716-729`, and a pool keyed by a 4-tuple annotated as a 3-tuple (`adapters/tts/greeting_pool.py:39`); session-keyed module globals in `history_sync.py:13,18`, `realtime_error_alerts.py:29`, `ws_relay_session.py:128,174` are cleared only in `lifecycle._finalize:1568`, which explicit `end_call` paths skip.
- **Fuzzy `LIKE` session lookup and free-form status** — `src/components/voice/repositories.py:259-269` matches a session by string id with `LIKE`, so a wrong row can match; `end_session` writes a caller-supplied status straight into the Postgres enum column (`src/components/voice/schemas.py:49`; `repositories.py:206`; `models.py:32-44`).
- **Transcript rule contradictions** — `'nope'` is both a substantive short answer (`src/components/voice/session/agent.py:1001-1008`) and a hard-skip phrase checked first (`src/components/voice/constants/fillers.py:84-99`; `agent.py:906`); `'o'`, `'to'`, `'too'`, `'for'`, `'ate'` map to digits (`src/components/voice/transcript/languages/english.py:159-172`) and Hindi `'so'` maps to `'00'` (`hindi.py:463`); `is_valid_otp` ignores `expected_length` so `OTPCaptureProfile.otp_length` is meaningless (`src/components/voice/transcript/core/spoken_format.py:552-565`; `core/capture_fields/otp.py:12-23`); `should_play_filler()` would raise because `FillerGuard` is a Protocol without `@runtime_checkable` (`transcript/engine.py:245`; `guards/base.py:84`).
- **Timing regimes and hardcoded tuning** — legacy 700 ms / 3000 ms (`src/components/voice/constants/timing.py:17-18`, used by `decision.py`) vs 1200/1600 ms holds and 8000 ms max age (`src/components/voice/transcript/strategies/constants.py:15-34`); `timing.py:8-16` admits values "flip-flopped across several past fix: voice commits". Other hardcoded tuning: `MAX_ACTIVE_CALLS = 6` (`src/components/voice/worker.py:161`), `load_threshold=0.99` (`:762`), `/tmp/prometheus_multiproc_voice_worker` (`:772`); `_prewarm_llm_with_context` runs a real LLM call with `bypass_db=True` under an explicit "verify on first test" assumption (`src/components/voice/session/lifecycle.py:1021-1116`, not re-verified).

## 11.9 Campaign/CDR/jobs specifics

- **Beat entry targets a name that is never registered** — `sweep-expiring-credentials` schedules `src.components.integrations.tasks.sweep_expiring_credentials` (`src/makunai_celery/celery_config.py:100`) but the task is registered as `sweep_expiring_credentials` (`src/components/integrations/tasks.py:23`); every 15 minutes each worker logs "Received unregistered task" and drops the message (verified, venv `celery/worker/consumer/consumer.py:70-78`).
- **Lead-list repository and live sync are broken** — `CampaignException` is raised at `src/components/campaign/repositories/lead_list.py:102,119,140,144` but never imported (`:1-8`). `sweep_live_sync_lead_lists` can never sync: `LeadListService.sync_lead_list` raises when `get_request_auth()` is empty, which is always the case inside a worker (`src/components/campaign/tasks.py:185-193`; `services/lead_list.py:224-248`); line `:248` is unreachable after the raise; the task also instantiates a second `Container()` per run whose engine is never disposed (`tasks.py:178`).
- **`max_retries` without retry** — `write_invocation_log` (`src/components/orchestration/tasks.py:17,35-37`, comment `raise  # trigger retry`) and `process_document_ingestion` (`src/components/rag/tasks.py:25,201-207`) set `max_retries` but are not `bind=True`, never call `self.retry` and set no `autoretry_for`, so failures are final (verified, venv `celery/app/autoretry.py:9-36`).
- **Routing and registration quirks** — the glob `'src.makunai_celery.tasks.*'` (`src/makunai_celery/celery_config.py:19`) matches no task because tasks keep their defining module's name (verified, `celery/utils/imports.py:122` `gen_task_name`, called from `celery/app/base.py:600,650-651`); `common_notifications_queue` is declared but never routed to (`src/makunai_celery/celery_app.py:24`); `autodiscover_tasks` points at a module, not a package, so registration works only as a side effect of importing the hub (`celery_config.py:14-16`; `celery_utils.py:42`); `__all__` in `src/makunai_celery/tasks.py:20-33` omits `persist_session_fact` and `scheduled_campaign_start`.
- **Three event-loop strategies and an un-initialised Redis resource** — new loop + `set_event_loop(None)` (campaign, rag), `asyncio.run` (sessions, integrations, orchestration) and `asyncio.get_event_loop().run_until_complete` (`src/components/context/tasks.py:57,90`) share one prefork worker; after the first two run in a child, `get_event_loop()` raises on Python 3.12+ policies (verified on 3.14; 3.11 image not run); `celery.container.redis_pool` is a `providers.Resource` whose async initializer is never awaited in workers (`src/core/container.py:903-905`), and only the campaign `_run` harness overrides it (`src/components/campaign/tasks.py:22-39`).
- **Celery inside the API process and a 60-hour result TTL** — `session_memory` imports `context.tasks` at module level (`src/components/context/session_memory.py:8,147`), so the FastAPI process builds the Celery app and producers call blocking `.delay()` inside async handlers (`src/components/tools/executor.py:1478`; `src/components/rag/services.py:114,342,514,544`); only `HistoryManager` offloads to a thread (`src/components/history_manager/manager.py:157-163`). `result_expires = 60*60*60` (`src/makunai_celery/celery_config.py:36`) with a backend enabled (`celery_app.py:16`), so every 10-second coordinator tick leaves a `celery-task-meta-*` key; nothing reads results.
- **Lease acquisition defaults to acquired on Redis errors** — broad `except Exception: pass` around the per-campaign lease, so two ticks may run concurrently when Redis is degraded (`src/components/campaign/tasks.py:260-261,281-282,287-290,332-337`); `scheduled_campaign_start` calls `start_campaign(campaign_id, partner_id=None, actor_user_id=None)`, bypassing the ownership check by design (`tasks.py:525-527`; `services/campaign.py:499-502`).
- **RCS and API fallback handlers are dead** — only WHATSAPP is registered (`src/components/campaign/services/fallback_engine.py:28-30`); `container.py:1230-1237` injects rcs/api handlers that are ignored; the RCS Redis key format is incompatible with what `webhooks.py:63-77` reads (`node_handlers/rcs.py:67-68`); `node_handlers/` has no `__init__.py`.
- **Phone, retry and CDR mapping** — recipient import keeps a leading `+` (`src/components/campaign/services/lead_list.py:43-47`) while the Tata CDR mapper strips to digits (`services/tata_cdr_mapper.py:38-43`) and `get_dialing_by_partner_did_phone` compares by equality (`repositories/recipient.py:125`); `max_retries` actually means max attempts (`attempt_count + 1 >= campaign.max_retries`, `services/disposition_mapping.py:299,396,455`) and the schema allows 0 (`schemas/campaign.py:42`). CDR fields are never populated on the live path: `HollerCdrWebhookPayload` is built without `call_date_time`, `hangup_by`, `notes` (`src/components/campaign/services/campaign.py:1259-1276`); `src/components/cdr/models.py:17-20` says holler keeps no CDRs while `src/components/cdr/services.py:12-14` and `docs/architecture/07_PSTN_SIP_RELAY.md:27,54` say it does.
- **Console Bearer required for background paths** — DID claim/release silently no-op without a user token (`src/components/campaign/services/did_selection.py:72-78,113-119,154-160`); the babbler proxy and lead-list import also require a console session (`services/babbler_client.py:73-78`).
- **Session sweeper duplication** — thresholds exist as `ENV.CHAT_SESSION_*` (used by the task, `src/components/sessions/tasks.py:41-42`) and as `SWEEPER_*` constants (used for Redis TTLs, `src/components/sessions/constants.py:19-20`; `src/components/context/hot_tier.py:22`); the sweeper builds its own engine and Redis pool per run and closes sessions by `session_id` only (`tasks.py:31-38,66-68`); `_async_wrapper`'s comment about `pool.close()` is stale and `close()` is deprecated in redis 5.3.1 (`src/components/campaign/tasks.py:52-55`; `src/core/redis.py:30`; verified, venv `redis/asyncio/client.py:588-609`).

## 11.10 RAG/skin/prompt-builder specifics

- **"Universal chunks" cannot be retrieved** — the vector query `INNER JOIN`s `makun_ai_agent_documents` (`src/components/rag/repositories.py:617`; corrected from the survey's `:626-628`), so documents with no agent mapping yield zero rows although `ports.py:31`, `schemas.py:98-102` and `docs/architecture/02_COMPONENT_DEEP_DIVE.md:706` promise them.
- **Keyword boost outside the documented range** — full-query `%query%` ILIKE scores 2.0 (range documented 0-1); aggregation mode triggers on common words and fetches `top_k*3 = 300` full rows; `TOP_K == DIVERSITY_K` makes the diversity cap a no-op (`src/components/rag/repositories.py:609-635,650-652`; `constants.py:57-78`; `retrieval/knowledge_base_retriever.py:96-97,135,192`).
- **Synchronous Redis and HTTP inside async code** — module-level sync `redis.from_url` clients in `src/components/rag/services.py:51` and `tasks.py:22`; a 1-second SSE polling loop with no disconnect check (`services.py:414-454`); `requests.get` inside the worker coroutine (`tasks.py:68-73`); the PDF parser makes a synchronous `gpt-4o-mini` vision call per image page with model and prompt hardcoded (`src/components/rag/ingestion/parsers/pdf_parser.py:51-88`).
- **Progress and SSE lifecycle bugs** — the `PAUSED` status string is interpolated unquoted (`src/components/rag/services.py:485-487` vs `:452`); pause flags are set for the OLD task id while resume dispatches a NEW one (`:477-479,512-521`); cancel revokes with `terminate=True`, killing the worker child (`:465`). Progress stages EMBEDDING (80) and SAVING_TO_DB (90) are defined but the worker only writes STARTING, READING_BYTES, PARSING, CHUNKING and terminal states (`src/components/rag/tasks.py:83-86,139-149`; `constants.py:14-21`); `last_processed_page` is repurposed as an array index so resume re-parses the whole file (`tasks.py:172`).
- **Chunker and parser mismatches** — the `len(pages) > 1` branch returns rows unsplit, but the worker always calls `chunk([page])`, so long CSV/Excel rows are split despite the docstring (`src/components/rag/ingestion/chunkers/recursive_text_chunker.py:44-48` vs `tasks.py:172`); chunk size is 500 characters, not tokens (verified, venv `langchain_text_splitters/base.py:35-37`); `ExcelDocumentParser` advertises `.xls` but openpyxl reads only `.xlsx` (`parsers/excel_parser.py:13,23,38`; `constants.py:97`, openpyxl not verified in venv).
- **Upload race and unhandled errors** — the single-upload duplicate check is read-then-insert, so the partial unique index can still raise after the asset is uploaded, and the file is held in memory twice (`src/components/rag/services.py:80-109`; `models.py:115-121`); assign/unassign/resume do not catch `ValueError`, turning "not found" into 500 while OpenAPI promises 404/400 (`src/components/rag/controllers.py:344-375,213-222`).
- **Exception constructors called wrongly** — `SkinAnalysisError(reason=...)` on a plain `Exception` with no `__init__` (`src/components/skin_analysis/services.py:337-339,345-347,442` vs `errors.py:8-9`); `guard_rendered_length` passes one string where `RenderedPromptTooLongError(max_chars, received)` is required (`src/components/prompt_builder/helpers.py:153-156` vs `errors.py:41-46`), and the whole `helpers.py` has no runtime callers; `MissingVariableError` is never raised and the service default `strict=False` disagrees with the schema default `strict=True` (`services.py:199,232-237`; `schemas.py:74-80`).
- **Bounding-box semantics contradict themselves** — schema docstrings say x/y are the left/top edge; the prompt, `_to_pixel_coords` and `_plausible_findings` treat them as the centre (`src/components/skin_analysis/schemas.py:18-23,45`; `constants.py:225,241`; `image_annotation_renderer.py:378-386`; `services.py:637-638,673-674,687-690`); the renderer draws blurred ellipses, not boxes (`image_annotation_renderer.py:125,214-216`), and loads `arial.ttf`, normally absent on Linux (`:47-52`).
- **Simulation prompt, sweeper and hardcoded models** — `SIMULATION_PROMPT_TEMPLATE` requests a "clearly visible brightness and fairness lift" and a unit test enforces `'fair'` (`src/components/skin_analysis/constants.py:90-113`; `tests/test_simulation_prompt.py:35-41`). `fail_stale_simulations` has no beat entry and no caller (`src/components/skin_analysis/repositories.py:288-329`; `src/makunai_celery/celery_config.py:49-104`); simulation runs as an in-process FastAPI `BackgroundTask` (`controllers.py:389-413`); vision `gpt-4.1` and simulation `gpt-image-2` are hardcoded (`constants.py:19-20`; `engines/gpt_image_simulation_engine.py:30`), `SkinScan.model` persists the constant rather than the engine's model (`services.py:565`), and `images.edit` parameters for that model are not verifiable.
- **Prompt builder hardcodes and 500s** — `DEFAULT_BUILDER_MODEL`/`DEFAULT_BUILDER_TOKENS` are imported but `'gpt-4o-mini'` and `2048` are hardcoded and `enhance` ignores any model choice (`src/components/prompt_builder/services.py:296-298,325-327`); an invalid category raises `ValueError` from the enum setter, caught as generic Exception, so it becomes 500 (`models.py:143-147`; `controllers.py:178-180,215-220`); SSE endpoints always answer 200 and report validation failures only inside the stream (`controllers.py:69-79,339-362`).

## 11.11 Contradictions with docs/architecture/

Each item is "doc claim vs code reality". Doc line numbers come from the surveys; the ones marked verified were re-read on 2026-10-10.

- **WhatsApp inbound webhook does not exist** — `docs/architecture/02_COMPONENT_DEEP_DIVE.md:493-508` ("WhatsApp Inbound Webhook (added in PR #21)", verified) and `04_API_CONTRACTS.md:701-729` (verified) describe `GET/POST /ai/v1/integrations/whatsapp/webhook`, and `01_SYSTEM_OVERVIEW.md` lists it as done. No such route exists in `src/` (grep for `whatsapp/webhook`, `hub.verify_token`, `X-Hub-Signature` returns nothing; verified). WHATSAPP_VERIFY_TOKEN and WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET are referenced nowhere outside `src/core/environment.py:126-127`. WhatsApp traffic reaches this service only through babbler-service: conversational turns as `POST /ai/v1/chat/message` with `interface=whatsapp` (`src/components/chat/services.py:684-689`) and campaign fallback replies on `POST /ai/v1/voice/webhooks/babbler-reply` (`src/components/campaign/controllers/webhooks.py:28`, campaign code that never enters the chat pipeline); `WA_GRAPH_API_BASE` (`src/components/chat/media/constants.py:73`) is used only for media download.
- **Overview status table is stale** — `01_SYSTEM_OVERVIEW.md:31` calls Celery "Scaffold, placeholder tasks" (verified) vs 14 real tasks, 8 beat entries, two dedicated queues and KEDA scaling; `01:5` says "50050 (gRPC)" but no server binds 50050 or 50051 anywhere in `src/` (only client default `CONSOLE_GRPC_PORT=50051`, `src/core/environment.py:13`); `01:24` says "OpenAI + Anthropic" while Groq and Gemini are also registered (`src/components/llm/factory.py:394-397`); `01:107-115` omits the two campaign workers (`docker-compose.yml:43-81`).
- **Table and collection names lack the prefix** — `01:255-283`, `02:22,312,462` and `04` name `ai_agents`, `ai_tools`, `agent_tools`, `ai_call_sessions`, `partner_configs`, `rag_documents`, `rag_vectors`, `chat_transcripts`, `summary_blocks`; real names are `makun_ai_*` since `alembic/versions/27728e9fa7fc_v10.py` (`src/database/constants.py:1-34`), the RAG tables are `makun_ai_pdf_registry`, `makun_ai_document_chunks` and `makun_ai_agent_documents`, and the Mongo collections are `makun_ai_chat_transcripts` and `makun_ai_session_summary_blocks`.
- **Integration docs: Fernet and named adapters** — `01:54` (verified) and `02:360,474` vs AES-256-GCM via `AESGCM` with a 12-byte nonce (`src/components/integrations/core/vault.py:9,81-94`, verified). `02:443-448` lists `GoogleCalendarAdapter`, `SlackAdapter`, `CalendlyAdapter`, `RazorpayAdapter` (verified); only `HttpGenericAdapter` and `McpAdapter` exist (`src/components/integrations/adapters/factory.py:17-20`); `04:611-625` shows `adapter_key 'GOOGLE_CALENDAR'` while valid keys are `HTTP_GENERIC` and `MCP_GENERIC` (`src/components/integrations/constants.py:90-91`).
- **Router table, folder tree, container tree and auth flow** — `01:323-336` lists 12 routers; `src/routes/base.py:40-64` includes 25 (sessions, attachments, tool_credentials, agent_tools, constants, prompt_template, voice_dnc, lead_list, fallback_flow, contacts, metrics, campaign, campaign_webhook, disposition are missing); `01:136-155` omits `campaign/`, `cdr/`, `sessions/`, `db_constants/`, `rbac/`, `email_service/`, `grpc_interceptor/`, `protos/`, `exceptions/`; `02` has no section for campaign or cdr; none of the 48 campaign-family routes appear in `04`; `01:325-326` mounts `agent_integration_router` at `/ai/v1/integrations` (actual `/ai/v1/agents/{agent_id}/integrations`). `01:183-227` lists `tool_output_compactor` (no such provider or file; grep for "compactor" finds nothing), calls `tool_service_clients` a Singleton (it is a `providers.Resource`, `src/core/container.py:992-994`) and uses provider names that do not exist (`knowledge_retriever`, `parser_registry` vs `knowledge_base_retriever`, `rag_parser_registry`, `container.py:1062-1096`); `01:236-251` lists 11 wired modules vs 27 (`container.py:863-892`); `01:339-358` shows `await container.tool_service_clients().initialize()`, which `src/main.py` never calls, and omits the `ConstantsCreator`/`ConstantsInitializer` steps (`main.py:77-78`); `01:72` labels auth "gRPC → Redis" while code checks Redis first (`src/middlewares/authentication.py:105-130`); `01:296-312` omits the `X-Agent-Secret` branch (`authentication.py:69-79,196-234`); `01:331` implies `/health` is open.
- **Chat routes, events, enum and mechanics** — `02:174-178` and `01:322` list `GET /ai/v1/chat/session/{id}/history`, `DELETE .../clear`, `GET /ai/v1/chat/sessions`; these live under `/ai/v1/sessions` (`src/components/sessions/controllers.py:20,22,47,69`) and `04:334,388` documents a `/clear` suffix that does not exist; all docs omit `POST /ai/v1/chat/session/{session_id}/tool-calls/{tool_call_id}/consent` (`src/components/chat/controllers.py:197-198`) and the SSE events `rag_context`, `tool_result`, `tool_consent_required` (`src/components/chat/constants.py:29-31`); `02:44` and `04:257` say the interface enum is `chat/whatsapp/pstn/telegram/email` with default `chat`; actual `web/whatsapp/pstn/telegram/email/iframe`, default `web` (`src/components/common/channel.py:12-20`). `02:74-104` (and `ChatService`'s own docstring, `src/components/chat/services.py:1-13`) place the SILENT/INJECT/TRANSFER/HANDOFF loop in `ChatService`; it runs inside `LLMEngine.run_turn` (`services.py:691`); `02:592,605` say the orchestrator submits to `CompressionService` when turns exceed `max_raw_turns`, but the trigger is `MongoHistoryManager.append_messages` on every append (`src/components/history_manager/manager.py:149-169`); `02:96-99` says the Mongo `$slice` window feeds the LLM, but the Redis hot tier is preferred when `HOT_TIER_ENABLED` (`src/components/context/context_orchestrator.py:185-200`); `02:137` and `03:94` say the attachment key is `chat_attachment:{asset_id}` (actual `makunai_media_constant:{asset_id}`, `src/components/chat/media/constants.py:63`); `02:593,607-611` describe a `ToolOutputCompactor` that no file implements.
- **LLM and orchestration docs** — `02:227-231` says the overflow budget is limit x 0.85 x 4 chars; code uses `TOKEN_OVERFLOW_THRESHOLD = 0.80` on token estimates (`src/components/llm/constants.py:137`; `core.py:1298-1301`); `02:281-285` cites `llm/static_methods.py` (helpers live in `src/components/llm/helpers.py`); `03:354` says `SubAgentRunner` runs `LLMEngine.run_turn` (it calls `LLMCore.generate_response_stream`, `src/components/orchestration/sub_agent_runner.py:394-456`); `03:357` says the runner fires `write_invocation_log` (`ToolExecutor` does, `src/components/tools/executor.py:1474-1489`); `04:1196-1199` documents `build_llm_request` as `async def` (synchronous, `src/components/agents/services.py:275,459`); `02:630` presents `had_tools` as the voice tool signal (never set); `02:562` calls `MissingPartnerApiKeyError` a hard stop (voice catches the wrong class and continues, `src/components/voice/session/lifecycle.py:114`); `03:309` says `LLMEngine` yields `ToolResultEvent` (it does not, `src/components/llm/engine.py:505-510`; `02:628` and `CHANGELOG:10` already say so).
- **Prompt builder and RAG docs** — `02:642-663` and `01:328` describe `GET /ai/v1/prompts/templates` and `POST /ai/v1/prompts/generate` with `template_id + business_details`; real routers are `/ai/v1/prompt-templates` and `/ai/v1/prompt-builder/generate|enhance` with `description/tier/tools/constraints` (`src/components/prompt_builder/controllers.py:57-64,300,364`); `02:652-657` names columns and an adapter class that do not exist (`schema_definition`, `system_prompt_template`, `LLMPromptBuilderAdapter` vs `variables`, `base_prompt`, `PromptBuilderLLMAdapter`). `02:682` and `04:909` say 20 MB (verified); code is 80 MB (`src/components/rag/constants.py:31,86`, verified); `02:691` says 500 tokens with 50-token overlap (characters); `02:695` lists an EMBEDDING stage the worker never writes; `02:706` promises universal chunks (see 11.10); `02:709-720` and `04:901-957` omit bulk upload, pause, resume and bulk assign/unassign routes (`src/components/rag/controllers.py:108,147,200,209,257,294`).
- **Skin analysis** — `02:727,758-759` and `03:306` describe a numeric 4/8/12-week heuristic in `static_methods.py` (no such file; trajectory is LLM text, `src/components/skin_analysis/schemas.py:113-124`); `02:736,746` name `LLMVisionSkinEngine` (actual `LLMVisionEngine`, `engines/llm_vision_engine.py:37`); `02:737,755` say boxes are drawn; `02:733-743` and `04:961-1062` omit `POST /ai/v1/skin-analysis/analyze` and `POST /scans/{scan_id}/simulation` (`controllers.py:209,309`).
- **Voice endpoints and pipeline** — `02:428-433` and `04:468-530` list four voice endpoints and omit `POST /ai/v1/voice/ws-ticket` (`src/components/voice/controllers.py:373`) and the WebSocket (`:403`); `02:378-387` and `03:168` show DTLN in the live pipeline (commented out, see 11.8); `02:375` says STT-LLM-TTS only, but SPEECH_TO_SPEECH and HALF_CASCADE realtime modes exist (`src/components/voice/pipeline/builder.py:135-153`); `02:410-417` says `run()` says the greeting and deletes the room on disconnect, but the greeting is spoken in `MakunVoiceAgent.on_enter` (`session/agent.py:538-650`) and `_finalize` never deletes the room (`lifecycle.py:1490-1572`); `01:229` omits the `livekit_runtime` provider that `voice_service` actually consumes (`container.py:1262-1272`).
- **PSTN relay doc and voice defaults** — `07_PSTN_SIP_RELAY.md:42` writes `wss /ai/v1/voice/stream` (verified); the route is `/ai/v1/voice/stream/{agent_id}` with `partner_id` and `token` query params; `07:28` calls the ticket single-use (verified) but no replay state exists; `07:230-233` lists GREETING_* env vars that bypass `ENV`; `07:125,139-142` tells operators to commit files into `assets/greetings/`, a directory absent from the repo; `07:63` says "Tata CDR webhook enriches by call id" without saying that makun-ai, not holler, maps fields and detects dispositions (`src/components/campaign/services/campaign.py:1162-1183`). `04:484,507` show `makun_sess_abc_f3a1b2c3`; actual format is `{call|call-int|call-local}_{session_id}_{8hex}` (`src/components/voice/constants/session.py:3-13`); `04:472-476` presents `session_id: "voice_test"` as usable; it is replaced by a generated id (`src/components/voice/services.py:38-60`); `04:525-530` maps `VoiceException` to 400 for all voice routes but `POST /sessions/{id}/end` returns 500 (`src/components/voice/controllers.py:305-311`).
- **Tool and integration docs** — `02:316-321` and `04:418-419` omit delegation mode APPROVAL and tool type READ (`src/components/tools/constants.py:39,49`); `02:361` says cache key `tool_credential:{id}` (actual `tool_cred:`, `src/components/tools/credentials/constants.py:9`); `04:395-411` describes pagination and INTEGRATION exclusion on `GET /ai/v1/tools` that do not exist (`src/components/tools/controllers.py:50-62`); `04:606-696` integration paths are wrong (`/discover-mcp` is `/mcp/discover`, `/templates/upsert` is `/templates`, `POST /credentials/razorpay` does not exist); `02:491` cites `integrations/model.py` (actual `models.py`); `02:458` says enable rejects duplicates (code reuses the row, `src/components/integrations/services/agent_integrations.py:207-241`); `04:1280-1290` signatures omit `context_data`, `force_execute`, `invoked_by` (`src/components/tools/executor.py:280-289`).
- **Wrong migration file names** — `01:260`, `02:44`, `03:580` attribute the channel-to-medium rename to `b798831ec8fa_v3`; the real v3 is `alembic/versions/1e6b2fb78419_v3.py`. `CHANGELOG.md:94` cites `bbbfd18605c6_v8.py` (actual `5d3fa585ee52_v8.py`); `CHANGELOG.md:31` cites `976f7c0c7b67_add_agent_invocation_log_tables.py`, which does not exist (tables created in `d94bf1acfc63_v17.py`).
- **Env reference, sessions and partner config** — `03:400-470` documents about 35 variables while `src/core/environment.py` defines about 120 (GROQ_API_KEY, GEMINI_API_KEY, GNANI_API_KEY, SARVAM_*, BABBLER_*, CDR_WEBHOOK_RELAY_SECRET, MAKUNAI_STREAM_SECRET, TTS_VOICE_ID, HOT_TIER_*, COMPRESSION_*, CHAT_SESSION_* missing); `03:415-416` lists CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS/ALLOWED_HOSTS as ENV attributes (they are `LocalSettings` fields, `src/config/settings.py:21-22`); `CHANGELOG.md:41` says end reason `idle_timeout` (code `'timeout'`, `src/components/sessions/constants.py:14`); `04:343,368` say list `limit` default 20 (`PaginationParams` default 50, `src/components/common/pagination.py:9`); `04:271` promises a no-op stream for an empty message (nothing enforces it); `02:525-528` says `rate_limit_enabled`/`idempotency_enabled` default False from ENV while the columns carry `server_default='true'` (`src/components/partner_config/models.py:54-65`); `04:744-848` omits `GET /ai/v1/partner/config/rate-limit-usage` (`src/components/partner_config/controllers.py:132`).
- **Transcript engine undocumented; env comment contradicts oc-config** — no architecture doc mentions `TranscriptEngine`, `TurnBuffer`, `PendingTurn`, `StabilityGuard`, `LanguageProfile`, `spoken_format` or `src/components/voice/transcript/` at all (grep by the survey); every "transcript" hit in the docs means the Mongo chat transcript. `src/core/environment.py:33-36` says the CDR_WEBHOOK_RELAY_SECRET default equals the oc-config value and that the cluster Secret overrides it; the dev Secret contains no such key (`oc-config/dev/makun-ai-env-secret.yaml:8-63`).

# 12. Verification notes

## 12.1 What was measured and how

All measurements were taken on 2026-10-10 against the checkout at `C:/Users/hp/Desktop/makun-ai-feature-dev-2026` and the ops repo at `C:/Users/hp/Desktop/makun-ai-oc-config-master`.

| Measurement | Result | Method |
|---|---|---|
| Files in the main repo | 648 | `find` over the working tree |
| Non-test `.py` files under `src/` (excluding generated `src/pub`) | 494 files, 90,519 lines | `find` + `wc -l` |
| Test files | 87 | `find` for `test_*.py` and `tests/` |
| Real folders | 108 in the main repo, 4 in the ops repo | `os.walk` (`scratchpad/folders_main.txt`, `folders_oc.txt`, `extract/folder_tree_counts.txt`) |
| HTTP route decorators | 146 HTTP + 1 WebSocket across 28 `APIRouter` objects | Python `ast` walk over every `src/**/*.py` (`extract/inventory.json`); a plain grep gives 148 because `src/components/common/responses.py:273,429` hold example decorators in docstrings |
| Celery task definitions | 14 | `ast` walk for `@celery.task` / `@shared_task` |
| Beat schedule entries | 8 | read `src/makunai_celery/celery_config.py:49-104` |
| Alembic migrations | 34, linear chain `7badb275c08a` (v1) to `b3f1a9c4d2e7` (v34) | `ls alembic/versions` + reading `down_revision` of each file |
| Locked packages | 179 | `poetry.lock` |
| Per-package line counts | voice 22,719; campaign 11,093; integrations 9,276; llm 6,007; tools 5,920; chat 4,712; prompt_builder 4,329; agents 4,318; skin_analysis 3,774; rag 3,572; core 2,164; common 2,155; partner_config 2,012; db_constants 1,276; context 1,236; sessions 1,170 | `wc -l` per package, tests excluded |
| Submodule state | `src/components/digital_assets` empty; 16 non-test files import from it (15 under `src/` plus `alembic/env.py`); `grep -rlI digital_assets src alembic` lists 19 files because two migrations and `src/components/chat/media/schemas.py` only mention the name | `ls` + grep for `from`/`import` lines naming `digital_assets` |
| Byte-order marks | `src/components/common/pagination.py`, `src/components/sessions/controllers.py` | `head -c 3` piped to `od` |
| Windows import failure | `from curses.ascii import US` at `src/core/environment.py:1` raises `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named '_curses'` | reproduced in the scratch venv (Python 3.14.7, Windows) |

Survey method: twelve reader agents each took one slice (infra-config, core-runtime, agents-sessions-partnerconfig-dbconstants, chat-context-history, llm-orchestration, tools-integrations, voice-core, voice-session-adapters, voice-transcript, campaign-cdr, rag-skin-promptbuilder, celery-jobs) and read every non-test file in it in full, recording per file its purpose, kind, difficulty, importance and imports, plus entry points, external services, data stores, key classes, library features, risks and contradictions with `docs/architecture/`. Their output is in `scratchpad/surveys/<slice>.json` and merged in `scratchpad/extract/*.json`. This section's author then re-read 40 of the cited lines with `sed -n` and corrected two (noted inline in section 11).

## 12.2 Library facts verified by reading source in the scratch venv

All paths below are relative to `scratchpad/venv/Lib/site-packages/`, where every package cited in this table is installed at its `poetry.lock` version except the ones noted in 12.3 (the virtualenv as a whole is not a faithful install of the lock; see 12.3).

| Library (locked) | Fact | Where verified |
|---|---|---|
| starlette 0.46.2 | `build_middleware_stack` wraps `ServerErrorMiddleware` outermost, then user middleware in list order, then `ExceptionMiddleware`, then the router (the loop iterates `reversed()`); so a request passes ContextMiddleware, CORS, AllowedHosts, Auth, ErrorHandling, RequestResponseLogging, then the route (`src/middlewares/__init__.py:30-43`) | `starlette/applications.py:79-99` |
| starlette 0.46.2 | `BaseHTTPMiddleware.__call__` passes any scope whose type is not `"http"` straight through; AllowedHosts, Auth, ErrorHandling and RequestResponseLogging all subclass it, so none run for the WebSocket | `starlette/middleware/base.py:93-101` |
| starlette 0.46.2 | `CORSMiddleware` also skips non-http scopes | `starlette/middleware/cors.py:76` |
| starlette 0.46.2 | `ServerErrorMiddleware` sends a 500 and re-raises; `ExceptionMiddleware` turns `HTTPException` into a plain-text response | `starlette/middleware/errors.py:150-187`; `starlette/middleware/exceptions.py:17-67` |
| starlette 0.46.2 | `StreamingResponse` is the base used for SSE in `src/components/common/responses.py:425-461`; `Router` matches routes in registration order | `starlette/responses.py:212`; `starlette/routing.py:656` |
| starlette-context 0.3.6 | `RawContextMiddleware` handles both `http` and `websocket` scopes, so it is the only project middleware that runs for the WebSocket; `RequestIdPlugin` reads `X-Request-ID` and generates a uuid4 when absent | `starlette_context/middleware/raw_middleware.py:15-110` (scope check at `:71-76`); `plugins/request_id.py` |

| fastapi 0.115.14 | `FastAPI(**extra)` stores unknown kwargs in `app.extra`, which is how `websocket.app.extra['container']` works (`src/components/voice/controllers.py:421`); `app.openapi()` builds and caches the schema | `fastapi/applications.py:813,841,966` |
