## 12.3 Version caveats

- **asyncpg**: `poetry.lock` pins 0.30.0; the scratch venv has 0.31.0 because 0.30.0 has no wheel for the venv's Python 3.14. No asyncpg source was cited in this map, so no claim depends on the difference.
- **pillow**: `poetry.lock` pins 10.4.0; the scratch venv has 11.3.0 for the same reason. The Pillow calls cited for `src/components/skin_analysis/image_annotation_renderer.py` (`GaussianBlur`, `alpha_composite`, `thumbnail`, `ImageFont.truetype`) were read in 11.3.0 (`PIL/ImageFilter.py:173`; `PIL/Image.py:1824,2704,3587`; `PIL/ImageFont.py:803,1094`); they are long-standing APIs but were not re-checked against 10.4.0. One survey (chat-context-history) ran before Pillow was installed and recorded "PIL not installed" for `src/components/chat/media/preprocessor.py`.
- **livekit-api**: locked 1.2.0, venv 1.2.1; the two methods cited are present in both (not re-checked against 1.2.0). **prometheus-client**: locked 0.25.0, venv 0.26.0; only constructor and `labels` signatures were read. **grpcio**: locked 1.80.0, venv 1.84.0; the interceptor contract cited in 12.2 was read in 1.84.0.
- **The virtualenv as a whole**: comparing every `[[package]]` block in `poetry.lock` with the `*.dist-info` folder names in the venv gives 79 version differences out of 179 locked packages and 8 locked packages with no dist-info folder at all (`grpcio-tools`, `importlib-metadata`, `langchain`, `mpmath`, `opencv-python-headless`, `setuptools`, `sympy`, `zipp`). Examples: huggingface-hub 2.2.0 (locked 1.10.1), transformers 5.19.0 (5.15.0), onnxruntime 1.31.0 (1.24.4), numpy 2.5.3 (2.4.4), langchain-core 0.3.86 (0.4.0.dev0). A library claim in this map is "verified" only when the package whose source was read is installed at its locked version; the five exceptions are the ones named in this section.
- **groq** is NOT a locked package. The Groq provider (`src/components/llm/providers/groq_llm.py`) reaches Groq through the openai SDK with `base_url` `api.groq.com/openai/v1`. There is no Groq SDK source to verify.
- **phonenumbers** is NOT a locked package; phone validation in `src/components/voice/transcript/core/spoken_format.py:544-546` is hand-written.
- **Python**: the Docker image is `python:3.11-slim-bookworm` and `pyproject.toml:8` allows `>=3.11,<3.15`; the scratch venv is 3.14.7. Facts that depend on the interpreter version (event-loop policy behaviour, `audioop` removal, `datetime.utcnow` deprecation) were verified on 3.14 and are marked as such; behaviour on the 3.11 image was not run.
- **opencv-python-headless 4.14.0.94** could not be imported in the scratch venv, so `cv2.FaceDetectorYN` claims are unverified (see 12.4).

## 12.4 Claims marked "not verified"

These library behaviours are stated in the surveys and in this map on the strength of reading the project code only. The library source was not opened, or the package could not be imported in the scratch venv. Each row says where the project relies on the claim.

| Library | Claim | Where used |
|---|---|---|
| fastapi 0.115.14 | Typed `Query(ge=, le=)`, `Form(None)`, `File(None)`, typed path params; route-level `dependencies=[Depends(...)]` guards; `HTTPException` re-raise ordering; `APIRouter()` + `include_router` + `Response(content, media_type)`; `Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_session)]` with an async-generator dependency | `src/components/agents/controllers.py:75-83,474`; `src/components/agents/secret_rate_limit.py:33`; `src/components/health/controller.py:10,125`; `src/components/security/robots.py:7-9`; `src/core/db.py:82-88` |

## 12.5 What this map does not cover

This document is Phase 1, the project map. It answers "what exists, where, and what is broken or inconsistent". It does not:

- Explain any single module in depth (class by class, method by method, with the data it reads and writes). That is Phase 2, the deep per-package documents, one per `src/components/*` package plus `src/core`, `src/middlewares` and `src/makunai_celery`.
- Trace a request or a call end to end across packages (for example, one chat turn from `POST /ai/v1/chat/message` through `ChatService`, `LLMEngine`, `ToolExecutor`, `MongoHistoryManager` and the Celery compression task; or one outbound campaign call from `dispatch_coordinator` through holler-service, the LiveKit worker, the Tata CDR webhook and disposition mapping). That is Phase 3, the flow documents.
- Say in which order a new developer should read the code, what to run first, or which tests to trust. That is Phase 4, the learning path; the surveys' `learning_order` fields in `scratchpad/surveys/*.json` are its raw input.
- Verify runtime behaviour. Nothing was executed except import checks and small library snippets in the scratch venv; no database, Redis, Mongo, LiveKit or external service was contacted. Claims about behaviour under load, under Redis failure or on the 3.11 Docker image are inferred from source only.
- Cover the contents of the `digital_assets` submodule (not checked out), the holler-service, babbler-service or console code bases (other repositories), or the generated gRPC stubs in `src/pub/` beyond noting their import style.
- Re-verify every one of the 366 survey risk items. 40 citations were re-read by this section's author; the remaining items in section 11 carry the survey's line numbers and the ones not re-read are marked "(not re-verified)".
